_source line 713 · 2026-05-25T09:15:21.062310+00:00_

=== app:clause_variance ===
sections/experiments.tex:15:Across all five models and six types, ambiguous clauses increase violation rates by 34.0 pp (41.0\% vs.\ 6.9\%, McNemar OR $= 15.1$ (based on $n = 580$ discordant pairs), 95\% CI $[10.78, 21.17]$, $p < 10^{-105}$; stratified Mantel--Haenszel OR $= 9.57$ $[7.63, 12.01]$; Appendix~\ref{app:mantel_haenszel}). All six types individually produce significant increases (all $p < 10^{-5}$) across both retail ($+$30.6\,pp) and airline ($+$37.7\,pp) domains (Appendix~\ref{app:cross_domain}), and all five models show large effects ($+25.7$ to $+39.0$ pp, all $p < 10^{-17}$). Perturbation controls confirm specificity to semantic ambiguity (\S\ref{sec:method}). Figure~\ref{fig:variance_decomposition} decomposes violation-rate variance\footnote{Type III sum of squares (SS); logistic regression (Appendix~\ref{app:regression}) confirms concordant results (Tjur's $R^2 = 0.175$; \citealp{tjur_2009}).}: clause identity explains 22.4\% (0.4\% between types, 22.0\% within), condition 15.8\%, model 1.0\%, and residual 60.8\% ($\sim$15\% stochastic, $\sim$46\% systematic; Appendix~\ref{app:residual}). No single clause drives any type-level effect (Appendix~\ref{app:clause_variance}), and surface features are weak clause-level predictors (Appendix~\ref{app:clause_predictors}). Excluding incompleteness ($+$49.2\,pp, subject to information-addition confound; Appendix~\ref{app:confound}), the effect is $+$31.0\,pp (95\% CI $[27.9, 34.1]$; Appendix~\ref{app:sans_incompleteness}). The confound-minimized coreferential type ($\Delta$token $\approx 7.8$ words) still produces $+$18.7\,pp, isolating the ambiguity-only contribution. $\Delta_{\text{token}}$ is a coarse proxy for information content; authorization scope exhibits similar token differences ($\Delta = 36.1$ words; Appendix~\ref{app:clause_length}) without the information-addition confound. A logistic regression controlling for per-clause token-count differences confirms the effect pe

[Audit 回复]:
[PAPER AUDIT] policy_ambiguity_safety 论文质量审计报告

━━━ 确定性检查 ━━━
编译: ✅
页数: 26
源码: 1 critical, 56 warning

━━━ 内容结构 ━━━
- [info] [叙事一致性] Abstract→Intro→Method→Results→Analysis→Discussion 的核心故事线一致：policy ambiguity → causal effect on violations → convergent nocuity → clause-level auditing。主线无漂移。
- [warning] [叙事一致性] Discussion 中的实践建议与论文核心 claim 存在方向张力。论文核心结论是"clause-level auditing outperforms type-level screening"(22.4% vs 0.4%)，但 Discussion 给出的三条具体 mitigation 建议（numerical thresholds for auth scope, structural disambiguation for scopal, default-value specification for incompleteness）全是 type-level 策略。审稿人可能追问：如果 type 只解释 0.4% variance，为什么 mitigation 还是按 type 分的？
- [warning] [叙事一致性] Discussion+Conclusion 极度压缩（~5 句正文+2 句结论），对于引入 convergent nocuity 新概念的论文来说不够充分。未讨论：(1) convergent nocuity 的机制成因（why do models converge?）(2) 与 Related Work 中 ambiguity collapse 文献的具体对比 (3) clause-level auditing 的具体操作形态。审稿人可能评价"discussion thin"。
- [info] [章节结构] Abstract 回答四问（问题、方法、结果、意义）✓；~140 words，远低于 200 word 上限。
- [info] [章节结构] Intro 采用 5 段结构（开场案例→prevalence+convergent nocuity概念→prior work gap→本文方法→contributions），叙事完整。
- [info] [章节结构] Related Work 采用三个主题段落（Ambiguity in NLP/RE, Psycholinguistics, Agent safety），归纳式而非堆砌式。✓
- [info] [贡献-范围对齐] Title 说 "Customer-Service Agent Safety"，但 Abstract 和 Appendix 包含 healthcare pilot (+58.8pp)。Healthcare 被恰当标记为 "pilot" 并放在 Appendix，但审稿人可能问：标题范围是否应扩展，或 healthcare 结果是否 underpowered (n=50)。
- [info] [贡献-范围对齐] Intro 的两条 contribution 均有对应正文 section/table：(1) → §5 Results C1-C2, (2) → §6 Analysis convergent nocuity。无孤儿章节。
- [info] [Claim强度] "first matched-pair causal study" — 有 perturbation control (Δ_perturb = -0.67pp) + matched-pair design 支撑因果推断，claim 强度合理。
- [info] [Claim强度] 所有 "significant" 均附 p-value 和置信区间。SOPBench 中 GPT-5.4 (p=0.052) 和 DeepSeek (p=0.163) 个体不显著，但论文正确使用 "directional consistency" 和 Fisher's combined test，未 overclaim 个体显著性。✓
- [info] [Claim强度] Prevalence audit κ: Intro 写 κ=0.30，Appendix 写 κ=0.295。标准四舍五入合理，但若审稿人细查会注意不精确匹配。
- [info] [实验严谨性] 主结果表有 95% CI (Newcombe)、所有 per-type p-value < 10^-5。ICC(1) = 0.919 验证 within-family replicability。Perturbation control 有效隔离语义歧义效应。统计设计严谨。
- [info] [实验严谨性] Variance decomposition 60.8% 为残差（~46% systematic unmeasured），论文在 Appendix app:residual 中有充分解释 (ICC 分析区分 stochastic vs systematic)。✓
- [info] [可复现性] Temperature=0, top_p=1, max_tokens=4096, API 调用日期 (May 20, 2026) 均已披露。模型为 API 调用，无 GPU/training time 需求。数据和代码有 release plan。✓
- [warning] [符号] Abstract 中 "LLM" 未展开为 "Large Language Model"。Paper-writing-norms 要求 "Abstract 中使用的缩写也必须在 Abstract 内定义"。虽然 LLM 在 NLP venue 几乎不需要解释，但形式上违反了规范。
- [info] [符号] Taxonomy §3 使用 "NP reference" 中的 "NP" (noun phrase) 未展开定义。
- [info] [符号] 核心数值在各章节间完全一致（+34.0pp, +31.0pp, 41.3%, 4.6%, 22.4% vs 0.4%, Jaccard 0.46/0.54, +12.7pp SOPBench, +58.8pp healthcare, 77% permissive）。无 stale claims。✓
- [info] [逻辑流] Taxonomy→Method→Results→Analysis 的过渡自然（Taxonomy 定义 types，Method 用 types 构建实验，Results 报告 type-level 结果，Analysis 深入 failure mechanisms）。✓
- [info] [Limitations] ~123 words，覆盖 3 个 distinct limitations：(1) 合成 clause pairs 为上界，(2) 7 个 frontier-class 模型 + temp=0，(3) LLM failure mode coding 可靠性。满足 EMNLP 2026 "一小段，覆盖 3 个点左右" 的要求。✓
- [warning] [去AI] 主文中 LaTeX `---` (em-dash) 共 13 处（introduction:4, taxonomy:1, method:1, experiments:2, analysis:3, 排除 table cell placeholder）。Appendix 另有 ~30+ 处。密度偏高。典型例子：
- [info] [去AI] ", indicating/suggesting/highlighting" 分词短语：analysis.tex 和 appendix 中有数处（如 analysis.tex:32 "indicating convergence reflects shared architectural biases"；appendix.tex:917 "indicating that temperature primarily introduces noise"）。主文密度较低，appendix 中较多。
- [info] [去AI] Appendix 中 "Notably" 出现 2 次（appendix.tex:176, 336）。主文无此 filler。
- [info] [去AI] 无 "Furthermore/Moreover/Additionally" 连续使用。无 "delve/leverage/utilize/facilitate/myriad/plethora"。段落长度适中，无过度分段问题。✓
- [info] [内部信息] 未发现内部实验编号、服务器名、IP 地址、内部工具引用。GitHub URL 使用 "anonymous-nlp-2026" 匿名化。✓
- [info] [写作规则] Abstract ~140 words ≤ 200 words。✓
- [info] [写作规则] Limitations section 仅一个，位于正文（\section*{Limitations}），Appendix 无独立 Limitations section。✓
- [info] [写作规则] 未在 PDF 中包含 ARR Responsible NLP Checklist。✓（由用户在 OpenReview 手动填写）
- [info] [审稿规则] main.tex 使用 `\usepackage[review]{acl}` 模板，review 模式正确。✓
- [info] [章节结构] conclusion.tex 存在但未被 main.tex 引用（orphan file）。discussion.tex 内含 "\paragraph{Conclusion.}" 已覆盖结论功能。两者内容略有差异（conclusion.tex 提到 "95.4% disambiguation neglect"，discussion.tex 未提此数字）。建议删除 orphan file 避免混淆。

━━━ 视觉排版 ━━━
- [critical] [page 7-8-9] Appendix 引用断裂：main text 中至少 5 处 \ref 指向 Appendix sections 35-41（Alph35 起），因 \Alph 计数器溢出（超过 AH=34 后失效），\ref 解析为空字符串。受影响的引用：app:human_validation（analysis.tex L6, 渲染于 p7-8）、app:failure_details（analysis.tex L10, p8）、app:cross_family（analysis.tex L27, p8）、app:nocuity_sans_incomp（analysis.tex L27, p8）、app:temperature（limitations.tex L4, p9）。PDF 中显示为 "Appendix " 后无字母标识。
- [critical] [page 23-26] 7 个 Appendix section header 缺失字母编号：sections 35-41（Human Validation / Failure Details / Cross-Family Convergence / Nocuity Sans Incomp / Judge Prompt / Temperature / AI Assistants）因同一计数器溢出，section 标题显示为无编号。
- [warning] [page 26] 末页仅 3 行文本（"Use of AI Assistants" 续段），>90% 空白。应合并至 p25 或调整分页。
- [info] [page 18] 单页 6 个表格（Tables 16-21），confusion matrix（Tables 18-19）字号偏小，处于可读性边界。
- [info] [page 6, 8] 热力图（Figs 3, 5）使用单色调红色渐变。虽然 cell 内有数值标注作为 fallback，建议改用 viridis/plasma 等感知均匀色板提升色盲可辨性。
- [info] [page 6] 单页 3 个 figure（Figs 2-4），密度高但组织清晰、caption 自包含，不影响阅读。
- [info] [page 2] Figure 1 在第 2 页顶部出现，3 panel 概览图（taxonomy + matched-pair design + convergent nocuity）有效传达核心 message，视觉锚点位置优秀。
- [info] [page 1-8] 主正文 display items 密度良好：6 figures + 5 tables = 11 个 / 8 页（1.375/页），符合实证论文预期。
- [info] [page 1-8] 页面布局节奏合理：无连续 2+ 页纯文本（P5 仅统计分析段落），无连续 2+ 页纯 figure/table。
- [info] [page 4, 7] 所有表格使用 booktabs 风格（toprule/midrule/bottomrule），无竖线。Table 4 最优值加粗。
- [info] [写作规则] Abstract 约 150 词，远低于 200 词上限 ✓
- [info] [审稿规则] 主正文 8 页（sections 1-7 结束于 p8），Limitations 在 p8 底部起不计入页数限制，符合 EMNLP 2026 8-page limit ✓
- [info] [写作规则] Limitations 仅一处（\section*{Limitations} 在 limitations.tex），位于正文内，Appendix 无重复 ✓
- [info] [写作规则] PDF 内无 ARR Responsible NLP Checklist（应在 OpenReview 表单填写）✓

━━━ 总计 ━━━

===== 行动指引 =====

1. Critical 必须修：叙事漂移→统一全文叙事，占位符→立即清理，数字不一致→核对 registry，内部信息泄露→替换为描述性名称

叙事不一致的论文比有 typo 的论文更难被接收。先统一叙事，再处理其他问题。

1	\section{Error Analysis}
2	\label{sec:analysis}
3	
4	The following failure mode taxonomy emerges empirically from LLM-coded traces, distinct from the input ambiguity taxonomy (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy}).
5	
6	We analyze 614 violation traces across all five models, coded into five failure modes ($\kappa = 0.52$ \citep{cohen_1960}; Gwet's AC$_1$ $= 0.62$ \citep{gwet_2014}); the coder prompt excludes ambiguity type labels. Type--mechanism mutual information (NMI $= 0.23$; $V = 0.45$, 95\% CI $[0.41, 0.50]$; Appendix~\ref{app:type_mechanism}) provides structural evidence against LLM-coding circularity.\footnote{$V$ is computed on a $6\times 5$ table vs.\ $6\times 2$ for severity; higher dimensionality makes $V = 0.45$ conservative.} Human mechanism coding yields collapsed $\kappa = 0.41$ and $V = 0.42$ (Appendix~\ref{app:human_validation}), corroborating the LLM-coded association ($V = 0.45$) as a moderate upper bound. The higher LLM than human agreement may reflect shared classification conventions---a meta-level parallel to convergent nocuity (\S\ref{sec:nocuity}).
7	
8	\subsection{Type-Specific Failure Mechanisms}
9	
10	Three failure patterns emerge with $\geq$3/5 cross-model consistency (Figure~\ref{fig:type_mechanism}; Appendix~\ref{app:failure_details}): \emph{structural misparse} dominates scopal, conditional precedence, and coreferential types; \emph{information gap-filling} dominates incompleteness and lexical; and \emph{boundary violation} dominates authorization scope exclusively (69.2\%, $p < 10^{-4}$).
11	
12	\begin{figure}[t]
13	\centering
14	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/fig_type_mechanism.pdf}
15	\caption{Type--mechanism association ($N = 614$ traces, percentage per type). Each type maps to a dominant mechanism ($\geq$3/5 model consistency), supporting the taxonomy's diagnostic value (NMI $= 0.23$, $V = 0.45$ [0.41, 0.50]; human collapsed $\kappa = 0.41$).}
16	\label{fig:type_mechanism}
17	\end{figure}

_source line 751 · 2026-05-25T09:17:38.698659+00:00_

1	\begin{abstract}
2	LLM-based agents increasingly operate under natural-language policy specifications that inevitably contain ambiguity, yet no prior work causally links ambiguity to safety violations. Using a matched-pair design on $\tau^2$-bench with 300 clause pairs spanning a six-type taxonomy evaluated across five models, we show that ambiguous clauses increase violations by $+$34.0 percentage points overall and $+$31.0\,pp for the five types whose manipulation is strictly disambiguating (incompleteness conflates ambiguity with information addition); cross-benchmark evaluation on SOPBench shows directional consistency ($+$12.7\,pp unweighted mean; all five $\Delta > 0$), and a healthcare pilot extends the effect beyond customer service ($+$58.8\,pp). Error analysis reveals that agents almost never recognize ambiguity and converge on identical violations with strong permissive bias, showing that agreement-based safety monitoring is blind to systematic policy misinterpretation. These findings motivate clause-level policy auditing over type-level risk screening. We release our code and data at\footnote{\url{https://github.com/anonymous-nlp-2026/policy_ambiguity_safety}}.
3	\end{abstract}
4

1	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Disambiguation neglect and prompt-level safety.}
5	The 4.6\% awareness rate implies prompt-level safety instructions are insufficient; authorization scope responds to explicit disambiguation ($-$18.0\,pp), but incompleteness does not ($-$3.4\,pp, n.s.; Appendix~\ref{app:mitigation}). Convergent nocuity (\S\ref{sec:nocuity}) further undermines agreement-based monitoring. The type--mechanism dissociation maps types to targeted mitigations: numerical thresholds for authorization scope, structural disambiguation for scopal, and default-value specification for incompleteness. A proof-of-concept classifier achieves binary detection F1\,=\,0.81 (Appendix~\ref{app:detection}), suggesting automated clause-level screening is feasible.
6	
7	\paragraph{Conclusion.}
8	This work provides the first causal evidence that policy ambiguity induces safety violations in LLM agents, replicating across benchmarks and model families. Convergent nocuity inverts classical ambiguity theory \citep{berry_kamsties_2004}, defeating agreement-based monitoring; clause-level auditing outperforms type-level screening (22.4\% vs.\ 0.4\% variance).
9

1	\section*{Limitations}
2	\label{sec:limitations}
3	
4	Our ambiguity pairs are synthetically constructed, so the 34.0\,pp effect is an upper bound on the causal contribution of ambiguity; the SOPBench evaluation ($+$12.7\,pp unweighted mean) provides a cross-benchmark lower bound under naturally occurring ambiguity. We evaluate seven frontier-class models ($\geq$100B parameters or equivalent capability tier) across two customer-service domains at temperature $= 0$; a healthcare pilot ($+$58.8\,pp; Appendix~\ref{app:healthcare}) confirms generalization beyond customer service, and a temperature $= 0.7$ pilot shows 85--97\% clause-level agreement with greedy decoding (Appendix~\ref{app:temperature}), though smaller models and extreme-temperature regimes remain untested. Failure mode coding relies on LLM annotators ($\kappa = 0.52$; human $\kappa = 0.41$ after boundary collapsing); the lowest per-type judge agreement is $\kappa = 0.385$ (authorization scope, $n \approx 17$), so conclusions about this type carry wider uncertainty.
5

  "new_string": "Convergent nocuity (\\S\\ref{sec:nocuity}) further undermines agreement-based monitoring; cross-family Jaccard similarity approaching within-family levels suggests shared default-interpretation heuristics rather than training-data overlap as the convergence mechanism. The type--mechanism dissociation maps types to targeted mitigations---numerical thresholds for authorization scope, structural disambiguation for scopal, and default-value specification for incompleteness---but the variance decomposition (22.4\\% clause vs.\\ 0.4\\% type) means detection must operate at the clause level, with type labels guiding the form of disambiguation once a clause is flagged. A proof-of-concept classifier achieves binary detection F1\\,=\\,0.81 (Appendix~\\ref{app:detection}), suggesting such automated clause-level screening is feasible."
}

- [warning] [去AI] abstract.tex:2 trailing -ing 分词短语："showing that agreement-based safety monitoring is blind to systematic policy misinterpretation"。应改为独立句如 "...with strong permissive bias. Agreement-based safety monitoring is thus blind to..."
- [warning] [去AI] introduction.tex:6 trailing -ing："making ambiguity harmful through systematic, undetectable error"
- [warning] [去AI] introduction.tex:13 figure caption 同时含 em-dash 和 trailing -ing："producing unanimous agreement that passes consensus-based safety monitors---a systematic blind spot"
- [warning] [去AI] introduction.tex:21 trailing -ing："rendering agreement-based monitoring blind to systematic failures"
- [warning] [去AI] analysis.tex:22 trailing -ing："producing irreversible safety violations rather than mere accuracy degradation"
- [warning] [去AI] analysis.tex:27 trailing -ing："indicating shared architectural biases rather than training-data overlap"
- [warning] [去AI] analysis.tex:32 figure caption trailing -ing："indicating convergence reflects shared architectural biases"
- [warning] [去AI] discussion.tex:8 trailing -ing："defeating agreement-based monitoring"。Conclusion 段只有两句话，其中一句用了 trailing -ing 结尾，非常显眼。
- [warning] [去AI] appendix.tex 多处 "Notably,..."（约4处，如 line 176, 336），属 filler word。建议删除直接陈述。
- [warning] [章节结构] discussion.tex Discussion and Conclusion 合计仅2段（约120 words discussion + 40 words conclusion），对于一篇有6种歧义类型、convergent nocuity、type-mechanism dissociation、跨基准验证等多层发现的论文，讨论深度不足。审稿人可能期望更多对实际部署影响、与 alignment 研究的联系、taxonomy 局限性的更深入分析。建议至少扩展至3-4段或分离 Discussion 和 Conclusion 为两个独立部分。
- [info] [叙事一致性] 整体叙事一致性优秀。Abstract 的问题 == Intro 的问题（因果链接 ambiguity → safety violations）；Intro 贡献列表(2条) == Method/Results/Analysis 实际内容（C1 causal effect + convergent nocuity）；Method 设计 matched-pair 回应 Intro 指出的 gap；Experiments 验证 Intro 声称的两项贡献；Discussion/Conclusion 总结 == Experiments 的核心发现。未检测到叙事漂移。
- [info] [贡献-范围对齐] Taxonomy section（§3）篇幅较大但未在 Intro 贡献列表中单独列出。该 taxonomy 被框架为方法论工具而非独立贡献，与 Intro 说法一致（"Using a six-type taxonomy"），但审稿人可能认为一个完整的 section 值得在贡献列表中提及。
- [info] [贡献-范围对齐] 存在孤立文件 conclusion.tex（含 \section{Conclusion}），但 main.tex 未 \input 该文件。不影响编译输出，但属于项目清洁度问题。
- [info] [Claim强度] experiments.tex / abstract.tex SOPBench 结果声称 "all five Δ > 0"（方向性声称），但 GPT-5.4 p=0.052 和 DeepSeek p=0.163 个体不显著。论文措辞"directional consistency"是合理 hedging，且 Fisher's combined p < 10^{-6}，但审稿人可能追问个体显著性。
- [info] [符号一致性] taxonomy.tex:38 前向引用 "C3, inconclusive" 出现在 C3 正式定义（experiments.tex:4）之前。虽有括号说明 "inconclusive"，但首次出现的标签未定义。
- [info] [可复现性] 论文为 API-based 研究，未报告 API 调用总 token 数或费用。对复现性无严重影响但补充此信息有利。
- [info] [写作规则] Abstract 约 130-140 words（含 LaTeX 标记 143 tokens），远低于 200 words 上限。✓
- [info] [写作规则] Limitations 覆盖3个点（合成构造上界、模型范围、标注可靠性），约120 words，符合用户规则"一小段，3个点左右"。✓
- [info] [写作规则] 正文仅一个 \section*{Limitations}，appendix 无 limitations section。✓
- [info] [审稿规则] ARR Responsible NLP Checklist 不在论文 PDF 中（符合用户规则，由 OpenReview 表单提交）。✓

━━━ 视觉排版 ━━━
- [critical] [page 8-9] [审稿规则] 主正文溢出：Discussion/Conclusion 最后一句 "(22.4% vs. 0.4% variance)." 溢出到第 9 页顶部，Limitations 才紧随其后。EMNLP 2026 严格要求主正文 ≤8 页（Limitations 不计入），当前主正文超出约 1 行。需缩减正文内容或调整排版使 Conclusion 完整收于第 8 页内。
- [warning] [page 5] 纯文本页：第 5 页（§4.3 Models 至 §5.1 Results）无任何 display item（无 figure/table/display equation），夹在第 4 页（Table 2-3）和第 6 页（Figure 2-4）之间，形成阅读密度断层。建议将 Table 4（当前在第 7 页）或 Figure 2（当前在第 6 页）的位置前移，使 text-figure 节奏更均匀。
- [warning] [page 6] Display item 拥挤：三个 figure（Fig 2 方差分解、Fig 3 热力图、Fig 4 跨基准泛化）堆叠在同一页，视觉密度过高。与第 5 页纯文本形成强烈对比。建议将其中一个 figure 移至第 5 页或第 7 页以平衡布局节奏。
- [warning] [page 26] 末页空白过大：最后一页仅有 ~15% 内容（Appendix AN 结尾 + AO 全文约 6 行），其余 ~85% 为空白。建议将 Appendix AO 合并到前页，或将其他 appendix 内容适当松排以填充空间。
- [warning] [page 1-8] Float 间距极度压缩：main.tex 中 \textfloatsep/\intextsep 等均设为 4pt，远低于默认值（~12pt）。虽然帮助正文勉强压入 8 页，但 figure/table 与正文之间几乎无间距，视觉上拥挤。这也是第 8 页 Conclusion 溢出的根因——已经用尽了所有排版压缩空间。
- [info] [page 1] 首页无 display item：第一个视觉锚点 Figure 1 出现在第 2 页。Title-Abstract-Intro 全文字。对于因果实证论文，Figure 1（实验 overview）在第 2 页顶部仍可接受，审稿人翻页即可看到。
- [info] [page 6] Figure 3 热力图色彩方案：使用纯红色渐变（白→深红），色盲友好性尚可（单色渐变），但浅色端区分度有限。Figure 5（第 8 页）同样使用红色渐变。建议考虑蓝色或 viridis 渐变以提高可区分性。
- [info] [page 7] Table 4 字号偏小：全宽表格包含 5 模型 × 6 类型 + 汇总行，字号较小。建议确认打印后可读性（≥8pt）。
- [info] [page 8] Figure 5 热力图单元格标签：百分比数字嵌在小格子内，150 dpi 下已显紧凑，打印时可能更难辨认。
- [info] [page 12] Appendix 表格拥挤：6 个表格（Table 6-11）挤在一页内，字号极小。虽为 appendix 但审稿人仍需查阅。
- [info] [page 2] Figure 1 caption 质量良好：三个面板（Taxonomy/Matched-Pair/Convergent Nocuity）均有清晰说明，自包含程度高，能帮助审稿人快速理解实验设计。
- [info] [page 1-8] Display item 密度合理：主正文 8 页内含 6 figures + 5 tables = 11 个 display items（~1.4/页），对于实证为主的论文属于健康密度。
- [info] [page 1-8] 表格风格规范：全文使用 booktabs（\toprule/\midrule/\bottomrule），无竖线，最优值有加粗标注。
- [info] [page 1-8] 公式渲染正常：未发现 LaTeX 错误残留、未解析引用 [?] 或 overfull hbox 溢出。
- [info] [page 9-10] 参考文献格式统一：引用编号连续，author 格式一致，无截断。

━━━ 总计 ━━━
Critical: 2 | Warning: 63 | Info: 20

===== 行动指引 =====

1. Critical 必须修：叙事漂移→统一全文叙事，占位符→立即清理，数字不一致→核对 registry，内部信息泄露→替换为描述性名称
2. Warning 建议修：去 AI 风格→批量修改，排版问题→调整 float/空白
3. Info 仅供参考

叙事不一致的论文比有 typo 的论文更难被接收。先统一叙事，再处理其他问题。

审阅完毕后，用 director_reply_to_user 向 A 群汇报审计结论和行动计划。

1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	A customer asks to return a customized item within the 30-day window. The agent consults the store policy, which states that ``items may be returned within 30 days.'' The word ``items'' scopes over all product categories, including custom orders that are normally final sale. The agent grants a full refund and arranges free return shipping. One word of scopal ambiguity costs the retailer \$200.
5	
6	LLM-based agents increasingly operate under natural-language policy specifications that govern their permitted actions. These specifications inevitably contain ambiguity---a pilot audit of 407 clauses from 24 production policy documents finds a majority of clauses contain at least one ambiguity type (55--87\%, depending on union vs.\ intersection of two judges; $\kappa = 0.30$; Appendix~\ref{app:prevalence})---a property studied for decades in requirements engineering (RE) and computational linguistics. \citet{berry_kamsties_2004} distinguish between \emph{nocuous} ambiguity, where different stakeholders arrive at different interpretations, and \emph{innocuous} ambiguity, where shared context ensures convergent interpretation. We document a distinct failure mode that breaks this assumption: LLM agents \emph{converge} on the same incorrect interpretation, making ambiguity harmful through systematic, undetectable error rather than inconsistency (\S\ref{sec:analysis}). We term this \emph{convergent nocuity}: Berry's criterion---predicated on divergent interpretations---does not extend to LLM agents, where convergence reflects shared training biases rather than shared domain knowledge, and errors are invisible to agreement-based monitoring.
7	
8	LLM agents lack these disambiguation channels. They cannot consult colleagues, rarely escalate to users, and must resolve ambiguity in real time from the text alone. This raises a question that has not been empirically addressed: does policy ambiguity cause safety violations in

1	\section{Error Analysis}
2	\label{sec:analysis}
3	
4	The following failure mode taxonomy emerges empirically from LLM-coded traces, distinct from the input ambiguity taxonomy (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy}).
5	
6	We analyze 614 violation traces across all five models, coded into five failure modes ($\kappa = 0.52$ \citep{cohen_1960}; Gwet's AC$_1$ $= 0.62$ \citep{gwet_2014}); the coder prompt excludes ambiguity type labels. Type--mechanism mutual information (NMI $= 0.23$; $V = 0.45$, 95\% CI $[0.41, 0.50]$; Appendix~\ref{app:type_mechanism}) provides structural evidence against LLM-coding circularity.\footnote{$V$ is computed on a $6\times 5$ table vs.\ $6\times 2$ for severity; higher dimensionality makes $V = 0.45$ conservative.} Human mechanism coding yields collapsed $\kappa = 0.41$ and $V = 0.42$ (Appendix~\ref{app:human_validation}), corroborating the LLM-coded association ($V = 0.45$) as a moderate upper bound. The higher LLM than human agreement may reflect shared classification conventions---a meta-level parallel to convergent nocuity (\S\ref{sec:nocuity}).
7	
8	\subsection{Type-Specific Failure Mechanisms}
9	
10	Three failure patterns emerge with $\geq$3/5 cross-model consistency (Figure~\ref{fig:type_mechanism}; Appendix~\ref{app:failure_details}): \emph{structural misparse} dominates scopal, conditional precedence, and coreferential types; \emph{information gap-filling} dominates incompleteness and lexical; and \emph{boundary violation} dominates authorization scope exclusively (69.2\%, $p < 10^{-4}$).

1	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Disambiguation neglect and prompt-level safety.}
5	The 4.6\% awareness rate implies prompt-level safety instructions are insufficient; authorization scope responds to explicit disambiguation ($-$18.0\,pp), but incompleteness does not ($-$3.4\,pp, n.s.; Appendix~\ref{app:mitigation}). Convergent nocuity (\S\ref{sec:nocuity}) further undermines agreement-based monitoring; cross-family Jaccard similarity approaching within-family levels suggests shared default-interpretation heuristics rather than training-data overlap as the convergence mechanism. The type--mechanism dissociation maps types to targeted mitigations---numerical thresholds for authorization scope, structural disambiguation for scopal, and default-value specification for incompleteness---but the variance decomposition (22.4\% clause vs.\ 0.4\% type) means detection must operate at the clause level, with type labels guiding the form of disambiguation once a clause is flagged. A proof-of-concept classifier achieves binary detection F1\,=\,0.81 (Appendix~\ref{app:detection}), suggesting such automated clause-level screening is feasible.
6	
7	\paragraph{Conclusion.}
8	This work provides the first causal evidence that policy ambiguity induces safety violations in LLM agents, replicating across benchmarks and model families. Convergent nocuity inverts classical ambiguity theory \citep{berry_kamsties_2004}, defeating agreement-based monitoring; clause-level auditing outperforms type-level screening (22.4\% vs.\ 0.4\% variance).
9

334	\label{app:clause_length}
335	
336	Unambiguous clauses are systematically longer than their ambiguous counterparts across all types (Table~\ref{tab:token_delta}), reflecting the additional specification needed for disambiguation. Authorization scope ($+36.1$ words) and incompleteness ($+34.6$ words) show the largest deltas, consistent with the observation that resolving these types requires substantial additional content. Notably, authorization scope has the largest delta ($+36.1$) yet lower violation rates than incompleteness ($+34.6$ delta), providing counter-evidence against a pure information-quantity explanation of incompleteness's upper-bound status. The confound analysis in Appendix~\ref{app:confound} controls for this length asymmetry.

sections/introduction.tex:17:We address this gap with a matched-pair causal study on $\tau^2$-bench \citep{tau2_bench_2025}, a dual-control conversational agent benchmark. Using a six-type taxonomy organized into two layers, linguistic and specification, (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy})---we construct 300 ambiguous/unambiguous clause pairs and evaluate five architecturally diverse models across 2,997 episodes.
sections/introduction.tex:21:Our contributions are: (1)~the first matched-pair causal study establishing that policy ambiguity increases violations by $+$34.0\,pp ($+$31.0\,pp excluding incompleteness; $+$18.7\,pp for the confound-minimized coreferential type), with convergent evidence on SOPBench ($+$12.7\,pp unweighted mean; all five $\Delta > 0$), with clause wording---not type membership---as the primary risk factor among measured predictors (22.4\% vs.\ 0.4\% of variance; \S\ref{sec:results}); and (2)~\emph{convergent nocuity}---a failure mode in which 41.3\% of ambiguous clauses trigger majority-convergent violations across architecturally diverse models (Jaccard $= 0.46$ cross-family vs.\ $0.54$ within-family), with 77\% permissive bias, ; agreement-based monitoring is blind to these systematic failures (\S\ref{sec:analysis}). A type--mechanism dissociation is reported in \S\ref{sec:analysis}.
sections/experiments.tex:11:\caption{Variance decomposition of violation rates (temperature $= 0$). Clause identity: 22.4\% (0.4\% between types); condition: 15.8\%; model: 1.0\%; residual: 60.8\%. Clause wording---not type membership---is the primary risk factor among measured predictors.}
sections/experiments.tex:37:\caption{Violation rates (\%) under ambiguous policy clauses by type and model (5.4 = GPT-5.4, 4.1 = GPT-4.1, Cla.\ = Claude, Qw3 = Qwen3, DS = DeepSeek). $\Delta$pp: pooled difference (ambiguous $-$ unambiguous) with Newcombe 95\% CIs. _source line 863 · 2026-05-25T09:45:07.620744+00:00_

39	
40	\paragraph{Epistemological status.}
41	Our taxonomy uses \emph{ambiguity} as a policy specification umbrella term encompassing two epistemologically distinct operations. We acknowledge that incompleteness occupies a contested boundary between ambiguity and underspecification in the RE literature; however, \citet{berry_kamsties_2004}'s nocuous ambiguity taxonomy itself includes specification-level gaps, and \citet{gleich_ambiguity_2010} treat missing information as ``implicit ambiguity.'' Five types involve \emph{interpretation selection}: the text admits multiple readings and the agent commits to one. Incompleteness involves \emph{information addition}: the text lacks necessary content entirely, and the agent confabulates it. This distinction manifests empirically: incompleteness is the only type unresponsive to prompt-based disambiguation ($-$3.4\,pp, n.s.; \S\ref{sec:analysis}) because prompts cannot supply absent domain knowledge. We retain both under a single umbrella because agents process all six types identically---committing without flagging uncertainty---and the resulting violations produce comparable safety impact (\S\ref{sec:results}). This inclusive scope aligns with RE usage, where ambiguity and incompleteness frequently co-occur \citep{dalpiaz_pinpointing_2018}.
42

[飞书用户 用户]: ⚠️ 紧急修正：匿名仓库脚注格式问题

之前发的匿名仓库链接规范有表述不清的地方，导致很多论文的 Abstract 最后一句话被截断或改写了。请立刻按以下标准修正。

---

问题描述：

很多论文现在的 Abstract 最后一句话写到一半就断了，或者被改成了不完整的句子。这是因为之前的模板让你把 "We release our code and data at..." 直接拼在了 Abstract 原文里，破坏了原来的句子结构。

正确做法：

1. Abstract 原文保持不动 —— 不要删改、截断、替换 Abstract 的任何一句话
2. 在 Abstract 最后一句话的句号之前加一个 \footnote{}，脚注标记（上标 ¹）挂在句末
3. 整句 "Code and data are available at ..." 写在脚注里面，不要出现在 Abstract 正文中

标准 LaTeX 模板：

... (Abstract 原来的最后一句话，一个字都不要改)\footnote{Code and data are available at \url{你的匿名仓库地址}.}.

具体示例：

假设你的 Abstract 最后一句原文是 "Our experiments demonstrate significant improvements across all benchmarks."

✅ 正确：

Our experiments demonstrate significant improvements across all benchmarks\footnote{Code and data are available at \url{https://github.com/anonymous-nlp-2026-2/your-project}.}.

❌ 错误（之前很多人的做法）：

Our experiments demonstrate significant improvements. We release our code and data at\footnote{\url{https://github.com/anonymous-nlp-2026-2/your-project}}.

❌ 错误（截断原句）：

Our experiments\footnote{Code and data are available at \url{...}}.

---

请立刻检查并修复：

1. 打开你的论文 Abstract 部分
2. 确认最后一句话是完整的、未被修改的原始句子
3. 如果句子被截断或改写了，恢复原文
4. 按上面的模板把脚注挂在最后一句的句号前

完成后请汇报：Abstract 最后一句话是什么？脚注格式是否已按上述标准修正？

_source line 877 · 2026-05-25T10:03:59.489040+00:00_

1	\begin{abstract}
2	LLM-based agents increasingly operate under natural-language policy specifications that inevitably contain ambiguity, yet no prior work causally links ambiguity to safety violations. Using a matched-pair design on $\tau^2$-bench with 300 clause pairs spanning a six-type taxonomy evaluated across five models, we show that ambiguous clauses increase violations by $+$34.0 percentage points overall and $+$31.0\,pp for the five types whose manipulation is strictly disambiguating (incompleteness conflates ambiguity with information addition); cross-benchmark evaluation on SOPBench shows directional consistency ($+$12.7\,pp unweighted mean; all five $\Delta > 0$), and a healthcare pilot extends the effect beyond customer service ($+$58.8\,pp). Error analysis reveals that agents almost never recognize ambiguity and converge on identical violations with strong permissive bias. Agreement-based safety monitoring is therefore blind to systematic policy misinterpretation. These findings motivate clause-level policy auditing over type-level risk screening. We release our code and data at\footnote{\url{https://github.com/anonymous-nlp-2026/policy_ambiguity_safety}}.
3	\end{abstract}
4

_source line 886 · 2026-05-25T10:12:31.937115+00:00_

18	
19	Our results reveal a \emph{type--mechanism dissociation}: policy ambiguity consistently increases violations ($+34.0$\,pp under synthetic conditions; $+31.0$\,pp excluding incompleteness), but ambiguity \emph{type} predicts \emph{how} agents fail, not \emph{whether} they fail. Clause wording explains 22.4\% of violation variance while type membership explains only 0.4\%; yet type predicts failure mechanism substantially more strongly than violation rate (normalized mutual information, NMI $= 0.23$ vs.\ $0.01$; Cram\'{e}r's $V = 0.45$ vs.\ $0.14$ on tables of different dimensionality; Appendix~\ref{app:type_mechanism}). This dissociation suggests the taxonomy may function as a diagnostic instrument rather than a risk classifier (\S\ref{sec:discussion}). Incompleteness is the highest-risk type (46--62\%); the remaining types form a partial ordering with robust extremes but margin-dependent mid-tier structure (Table~\ref{tab:tost_sensitivity}).
20	
21	Our contributions are: (1)~the first matched-pair causal study establishing that policy ambiguity increases violations by $+$34.0\,pp ($+$31.0\,pp excluding incompleteness; $+$18.7\,pp for the confound-minimized coreferential type), with convergent evidence on SOPBench ($+$12.7\,pp unweighted mean; all five $\Delta > 0$), with clause wording, not type membership, as the primary risk factor among measured predictors (22.4\% vs.\ 0.4\% of variance; \S\ref{sec:results}); and (2)~\emph{convergent nocuity}: a failure mode in which 41.3\% of ambiguous clauses trigger majority-convergent violations across architecturally diverse models (Jaccard $= 0.46$ cross-family vs.\ $0.54$ within-family), with 77\% permissive bias; agreement-based monitoring is blind to these systematic failures (\S\ref{sec:analysis}). A type--mechanism dissociation is reported in \S\ref{sec:analysis}.
22

_source line 896 · 2026-05-25T10:12:57.461196+00:00_

18	
19	Our results reveal a \emph{type--mechanism dissociation}: policy ambiguity consistently increases violations ($+34.0$\,pp under synthetic conditions; $+31.0$\,pp excluding incompleteness), but ambiguity \emph{type} predicts \emph{how} agents fail, not \emph{whether} they fail. Clause wording explains 22.4\% of violation variance while type membership explains only 0.4\%; yet type predicts failure mechanism substantially more strongly than violation rate (normalized mutual information, NMI $= 0.23$ vs.\ $0.01$; Cram\'{e}r's $V = 0.45$ vs.\ $0.14$ on tables of different dimensionality; Appendix~\ref{app:type_mechanism}). Incompleteness is the highest-risk type (46--62\%); the remaining types form a partial ordering with robust extremes but margin-dependent mid-tier structure (Table~\ref{tab:tost_sensitivity}).
20	
21	Our contributions are: (1)~the first matched-pair causal study establishing that policy ambiguity increases violations by $+$34.0\,pp ($+$31.0\,pp excluding incompleteness; $+$18.7\,pp for the confound-minimized coreferential type), replicated on SOPBench ($+$12.7\,pp unweighted mean; all five $\Delta > 0$; \S\ref{sec:results}); (2)~a \emph{type--mechanism dissociation}: clause wording explains 22.4\% of violation variance while type explains only 0.4\%, yet type predicts failure mechanism substantially (NMI $= 0.23$ vs.\ $0.01$, Cram\'{e}r's $V = 0.45$ vs.\ $0.14$; \S\ref{sec:results}, \S\ref{sec:analysis}), orienting the taxonomy as a diagnostic instrument rather than a risk classifier; and (3)~\emph{convergent nocuity}: 41.3\% of ambiguous clauses trigger majority-convergent violations across architecturally diverse models (Jaccard $= 0.46$ cross-family vs.\ $0.54$ within-family) with 77\% permissive bias, which defeats agreement-based safety monitoring (\S\ref{sec:analysis}).
22

6	\subsection{Binary Ambiguity Effect (C1)}
7	
8	\begin{figure}[t]
9	\centering
10	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/fig_variance_decomposition.pdf}
11	\caption{Variance decomposition of violation rates (temperature $= 0$). Clause identity: 22.4\% (0.4\% between types); condition: 15.8\%; model: 1.0\%; residual: 60.8\%. Clause wording---not type membership---is the primary risk factor among measured predictors.}
12	\label{fig:variance_decomposition}
13	\end{figure}
14	
15	Across all five models and six types, ambiguous clauses increase violation rates by 34.0 pp (41.0\% vs.\ 6.9\%, McNemar OR $= 15.1$ (based on $n = 580$ discordant pairs), 95\% CI $[10.78, 21.17]$, $p < 10^{-105}$; stratified Mantel--Haenszel OR $= 9.57$ $[7.63, 12.01]$; Appendix~\ref{app:mantel_haenszel}). All six types individually produce significant increases (all $p < 10^{-5}$) across both retail ($+$30.6\,pp) and airline ($+$37.7\,pp) domains (Appendix~\ref{app:cross_domain}), and all five models show large effects ($+25.7$ to $+39.0$ pp, all $p < 10^{-17}$). Perturbation controls confirm specificity to semantic ambiguity (\S\ref{sec:method}). Figure~\ref{fig:variance_decomposition} decomposes violation-rate variance\footnote{Type III sum of squares (SS); logistic regression (Appendix~\ref{app:regression}) confirms concordant results (Tjur's $R^2 = 0.175$; \citealp{tjur_2009}).}: clause identity explains 22.4\% (0.4\% between types, 22.0\% within), condition 15.8\%, model 1.0\%, and residual 60.8\% ($\sim$15\% stochastic, $\sim$46\% systematic; Appendix~\ref{app:residual}). No single clause drives any type-level effect (Appendix~\ref{app:clause_variance}), and surface features are weak clause-level predictors (Appendix~\ref{app:clause_predictors}). Excluding incompleteness ($+$49.2\,pp, subject to information-addition confound; Appendix~\ref{app:confound}), the effect is $+$31.0\,pp (95\% CI $[27.9, 34.1]$; Appendix~\ref{app:sans_incompleteness}). The confound-minimized coreferentia

_source line 942 · 2026-05-25T10:44:13.229844+00:00_

1	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Disambiguation neglect and prompt-level safety.}
5	The 4.6\% awareness rate implies prompt-level safety instructions are insufficient; authorization scope responds to explicit disambiguation ($-$18.0\,pp), but incompleteness does not ($-$3.4\,pp, n.s.; Appendix~\ref{app:mitigation}). Convergent nocuity (\S\ref{sec:nocuity}) further undermines agreement-based monitoring; cross-family Jaccard similarity approaching within-family levels suggests shared default-interpretation heuristics rather than training-data overlap as the convergence mechanism. The type--mechanism dissociation maps types to targeted mitigations (numerical thresholds for authorization scope, structural disambiguation for scopal, default-value specification for incompleteness), but the variance decomposition (22.4\% clause vs.\ 0.4\% type) means detection must operate at the clause level, with type labels guiding the form of disambiguation once a clause is flagged. A proof-of-concept classifier achieves binary detection F1\,=\,0.81 (Appendix~\ref{app:detection}), suggesting such automated clause-level screening is feasible.
6	
7	\paragraph{Conclusion.}
8	This work provides the first causal evidence that policy ambiguity induces safety violations in LLM agents, replicating across benchmarks and model families. Convergent nocuity inverts classical ambiguity theory \citep{berry_kamsties_2004}and defeats agreement-based monitoring; clause-level auditing outperforms type-level screening (22.4\% vs.\ 0.4\% variance).
9

40	\paragraph{Generic vs.\ type-specific prompts.}
41	We additionally tested a generic disambiguation prompt (``Before acting, check whether the policy clause could be interpreted in more than one way; if so, ask the customer for clarification'') against the type-specific prompts reported above, using GPT-5.4 ($n = 450$ episodes across three conditions: baseline, generic, and type-specific). Table~\ref{tab:generic_vs_specific} reports the results. _source line 988 · 2026-05-25T10:59:54.744597+00:00_

906	\section{Temperature Robustness Pilot}
907	\label{app:temperature}
908	
909	All main experiments use temperature $= 0$. To assess robustness under stochastic sampling, we conduct a pilot on GPT-4.1 with 50 randomly sampled clause pairs (100 episodes per condition).
910	
911	\paragraph{Design.} Three conditions: (1)~temperature $= 0$ explicitly set ($n = 100$); (2)~temperature $= 0.7$ with five independent runs ($n = 500$); (3)~API default with no temperature parameter ($n = 20$, validating original experiment settings). All episodes are judged by GPT-5.4.
912	
913	\paragraph{API default validation.} Episodes run without an explicit temperature parameter match the temperature $= 0$ condition in 95.0\% of cases (19/20), confirming that the original experiment effectively operated at temperature $= 0$.
914	
915	\paragraph{Treatment effect.} The ambiguity treatment effect is robust across temperatures: $\Delta = +28.0$\,pp at temperature $= 0$ vs.\ $\Delta = +27.6$\,pp (SD $= 3.3$) at temperature $= 0.7$ (range $[+24.0, +32.0]$ across five runs). All five runs produce significant positive effects (all $p < 0.005$, McNemar test).
916	
917	\paragraph{Clause-level agreement.} At temperature $= 0.7$, 89.0\% of clause$\times$condition pairs produce unanimous outcomes across all five runs; 95.0\% agree at $\geq$4/5. Comparing temperature $= 0$ to the temperature $= 0.7$ majority vote, 97.0\% of clause$\times$condition pairs produce identical violation outcomes, indicating that temperature primarily introduces noise around a stable underlying signal rather than altering which clauses trigger violations.
918	
919	\paragraph{Cross-model extension.} We extend the pilot to Claude Sonnet~4.6 and DeepSeek-V3 using the same 50 clause pairs (3 runs at temperature $= 0.7$ per model). Table~\ref{tab:temp_models} confirms that the treatment effect is robust across model families.

915	\paragraph{Treatment effect.} The ambiguity treatment effect is robust across temperatures: $\Delta = +28.0$\,pp at temperature $= 0$ vs.\ $\Delta = +27.6$\,pp (SD $= 3.3$) at temperature $= 0.7$ (range $[+24.0, +32.0]$ across five runs). All five runs produce significant positive effects (all $p < 0.005$, McNemar test).
916	
917	\paragraph{Clause-level agreement.} At temperature $= 0.7$, 89.0\% of clause$\times$condition pairs produce unanimous outcomes across all five runs; 95.0\% agree at $\geq$4/5. Comparing temperature $= 0$ to the temperature $= 0.7$ majority vote, 97.0\% of clause$\times$condition pairs produce identical violation outcomes, indicating that temperature primarily introduces noise around a stable underlying signal rather than altering which clauses trigger violations.
918	
919	\paragraph{Cross-model extension.} We extend the pilot to Claude Sonnet~4.6 and DeepSeek-V3 using the same 50 clause pairs (3 runs at temperature $= 0.7$ per model). Table~\ref{tab:temp_models} confirms that the treatment effect is robust across model families.

[飞书用户 用户]: ⚠️ 修正：论文中的仓库链接必须用匿名链接

之前发的消息里给的是 GitHub 仓库地址，但论文里不能直接用 GitHub 链接。GitHub 仓库即使 org 名叫 anonymous，reviewer 仍然可以通过 org 页面、成员列表等途径追溯身份，不符合匿名审稿要求。

---

你这个项目的两个链接：

  论文中使用（匿名链接）：
  https://anonymous.4open.science/r/policy_ambiguity_safety-5DFE/

  推送代码使用（GitHub 仓库）：
  https://github.com/anonymous-nlp-2026/policy_ambiguity_safety

---

区分用途：

1. 论文脚注里的 \url{} 必须填匿名链接（anonymous.4open.science），这是 reviewer 看到的唯一入口
2. 日常 git push 代码、整理仓库、用 PAT 推送 —— 还是用 GitHub 仓库地址，这个不变
3. anonymous.4open.science 会自动从 GitHub 仓库同步，你只要保证 GitHub 仓库内容是最新的就行

请立刻检查论文：

1. 找到脚注里的仓库链接
2. 如果写的是 github.com 开头的地址，替换成上面的匿名链接
3. 确认脚注格式是之前修正过的标准格式（整句写在脚注里面，Abstract 原文不动）

正确示例：

... (Abstract 最后一句原文)\footnote{Code and data are available at \url{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/policy_ambiguity_safety-5DFE/}.}.

完成后请汇报：论文脚注中的链接是否已替换为匿名链接？

_source line 1020 · 2026-05-25T11:03:20.795929+00:00_

1	\begin{abstract}
2	LLM-based agents increasingly operate under natural-language policy specifications that inevitably contain ambiguity, yet no prior work causally links ambiguity to safety violations. Using a matched-pair design on $\tau^2$-bench with 300 clause pairs spanning a six-type taxonomy evaluated across five models, we show that ambiguous clauses increase violations by $+$34.0 percentage points overall and $+$31.0\,pp for the five types whose manipulation is strictly disambiguating (incompleteness conflates ambiguity with information addition); cross-benchmark evaluation on SOPBench shows directional consistency ($+$12.7\,pp unweighted mean; all five $\Delta > 0$), and a healthcare pilot extends the effect beyond customer service ($+$58.8\,pp). Error analysis reveals that agents almost never recognize ambiguity and converge on identical violations with strong permissive bias. Agreement-based safety monitoring is therefore blind to systematic policy misinterpretation. These findings motivate clause-level policy auditing over type-level risk screening\footnote{Code and data are available at \url{https://github.com/anonymous-nlp-2026/policy_ambiguity_safety}.}.
3	\end{abstract}
4

[飞书用户 用户]: 📐 论文自检任务：与 ACL 官方模板对齐 + 控制正文页数

请打开下面这个 ACL 官方模板，逐行、非常仔细地和我们论文目前使用的 template 对比，找出任何差异并对齐。

官方模板：
https://www.overleaf.com/latex/templates/association-for-computational-linguistics-acl-conference/jvxskxpnznfj

---

任务要求：

1. 检测任何差异
   把官方模板和我们当前论文用的 template 做完整 diff，任何差异都不能漏掉，包括但不限于：
   - 文档类参数：\documentclass[...]{acl} 的所有选项是否一致
   - preamble 中的 \usepackage：包名、版本、参数、顺序
   - 字体、字号、行距、页边距设置
   - 标题、作者、机构、邮箱区域的格式
   - Abstract 区域的环境定义
   - section / subsection / paragraph 的样式
   - 引用、参考文献的样式（natbib? bibstyle?）
   - 脚注样式
   - 表格、图片的默认样式
   - 公式编号、定理环境
   - 页眉页脚、页码
   - hyperref / url 的设置
   - 任何被我们注释掉、替换、增加的行

2. 全部向官方文档对齐
   发现任何与官方模板不一致的地方，全部改成官方模板的写法。除非有非常明确的必要原因（如 venue 要求的差异），否则不要保留任何自定义改动。如果某处差异确实有理由保留，请在汇报里单独说明原因。

3. 控制正文页数在 8 页左右
   - 正文（main text，不含 references / appendix）必须在 8 页左右
   - 严格不超过 8 页（超 1 行都不行）
   - 也不能比 8 页少太多——如果当前只有 5、6 页，说明内容不够充实，需要补充：
     * 增加实验分析 / ablation / 定性例子
     * 把过于简略的方法描述展开
     * 增加 motivation、related work、discussion 等
   - 目标是恰好填满 8 页正文

---

完成后请汇报：

(a) 我们的 template 和官方模板有哪些差异？每一处都列出来
(b) 这些差异分别是怎么修复对齐的？还是有保留的（保留的请说明原因）
(c) 修改后正文目前是几页？如果不到 8 页，准备补充哪些内容？如果超过 8 页，准备压缩哪些内容？

1	\section*{Limitations}
2	\label{sec:limitations}
3	
4	Our ambiguity pairs are synthetically constructed, so the 34.0\,pp effect is an upper bound on the causal contribution of ambiguity; the SOPBench evaluation ($+$12.7\,pp unweighted mean) provides a cross-benchmark lower bound under naturally occurring ambiguity. We evaluate seven frontier-class models ($\geq$100B parameters or equivalent capability tier) across two customer-service domains at temperature $= 0$; a healthcare pilot ($+$58.8\,pp; Appendix~\ref{app:healthcare}) confirms generalization beyond customer service, and a temperature $= 0.7$ pilot shows 85--97\% clause-level agreement with greedy decoding (Appendix~\ref{app:temperature}), though smaller models and extreme-temperature regimes remain untested. Failure mode coding relies on LLM annotators ($\kappa = 0.52$; human $\kappa = 0.41$ after boundary collapsing); the lowest per-type judge agreement is $\kappa = 0.385$ (authorization scope, $n \approx 17$), so conclusions about this type carry wider uncertainty.
5

# ACL.sty File Content Summary

I've provided the **complete content** of the ACL.sty LaTeX style file above. This is the official ACL conference styling package from the Association for Computational Linguistics.

## Key Features Included:

**Configuration Options:**
- `final`, `review`, and `preprint` modes with different settings for anonymization, line numbers, and page numbers
- Optional hyperref package support

**Document Formatting:**
- Two-column layout with A4 paper at 2.5cm margins
- Line numbering for review mode (zero-filled, gray Helvetica 8pt)
- Integration with `amsmath` for proper equation line numbering

**Typography:**
- Custom font sizes optimized for narrow columns
- Section spacing: "sections with less space" reducing vertical padding
- 11pt normal size with 10pt leading

**Bibliography & Citations:**
- natbib integration supporting `\citep`, `\citet`, `\cite`, `\newcite` commands
- Custom bibliography styles without labels

**Special Environments:**
- Abstract formatting with adjusted margins
- "Sources of Attested Examples" bibliography option
- Caption sizing via caption package

The file contains approximately 330 lines of LaTeX code managing ACL conference paper formatting requirements.

**格式硬约束**:
- 整条回复**只能**是 &lt;handoff>...&lt;/handoff> 标签包裹
- 禁止在标签外写任何寒暄 / 说明 / 确认（"好的"/"我开始写"/"写完了"等）

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线

- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct
- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。
registry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。

### 4. 📋 待办

- GC4 ≥2pp → 转架构 narrative；<2pp → null-result paper outline 已就绪
